Revelation 19:5.

And a voice came out of the throne, saying, Praise our God, all ye his servants, and ye that fear him, both small and great.

Greek (Byzantine Majority Text)

Καὶ φωνὴ ἀπὸ τοῦ θρόνου ἐξῆλθεν, λέγουσα, Αἰνεῖτε τὸν θεὸν ἡμῶν πάντες οἱ δοῦλοι αὐτοῦ, καὶ οἱ φοβούμενοι αὐτόν, οἱ μικροὶ καὶ οἱ μεγάλοι.

Douay-Rheims

And a voice came out from the throne, saying: Give praise to our God, all ye his servants; and you that fear him, little and great.

Latin (Clementine Vulgate)

Et vox de throno exivit, dicens: Laudem dicite Deo nostro omnes servi ejus: et qui timetis eum pusilli et magni.

Arabic (Van Dyck)

وَخَرَجَ مِنَ ٱلْعَرْشِ صَوْتٌ قَائِلًا: «سَبِّحُوا لِإِلَهِنَا يَا جَمِيعَ عَبِيدِهِ، ٱلْخَائِفِيهِ، ٱلصِّغَارِ وَٱلْكِبَارِ!».

↩

Revelation 19:6.

And I heard as it were the voice of a great multitude, and as the voice of many waters, and as the voice of mighty thunderings, saying, Alleluia: for the Lord God omnipotent reigneth.

Greek (Byzantine Majority Text)

Καὶ ἤκουσα ὡς φωνὴν ὄχλου πολλοῦ, καὶ ὡς φωνὴν ὑδάτων πολλῶν, καὶ ὡς φωνὴν βροντῶν ἰσχυρῶν, λέγοντες, Ἁλληλούϊα· ὅτι ἐβασίλευσεν κύριος ὁ θεὸς ἡμῶν ὁ παντοκράτωρ.

Douay-Rheims

And I heard as it were the voice of a great multitude, and as the voice of many waters, and as the voice of great thunders, saying, Alleluia: for the Lord our God the Almighty hath reigned.

Latin (Clementine Vulgate)

Et audivi quasi vocem turbæ magnæ, et sicut vocem aquarum multarum, et sicut vocem tonitruorum magnorum, dicentium: Alleluja: quoniam regnavit Dominus Deus noster omnipotens.

Arabic (Van Dyck)

وَسَمِعْتُ كَصَوْتِ جَمْعٍ كَثِيرٍ، وَكَصَوْتِ مِيَاهٍ كَثِيرَةٍ، وَكَصَوْتِ رُعُودٍ شَدِيدَةٍ قَائِلَةً: «هَلِّلُويَا! فَإِنَّهُ قَدْ مَلَكَ ٱلرَّبُّ ٱلْإِلَهُ ٱلْقَادِرُ عَلَى كُلِّ شَيْءٍ.

↩

Revelation 19:7.

Let us be glad and rejoice, and give honour to him: for the marriage of the Lamb is come, and his wife hath made herself ready.

Greek (Byzantine Majority Text)

Χαίρωμεν καὶ ἀγαλλιώμεθα, καὶ δῶμεν τὴν δόξαν αὐτῷ· ὅτι ἦλθεν ὁ γάμος τοῦ ἀρνίου, καὶ ἡ γυνὴ αὐτοῦ ἡτοίμασεν ἑαυτήν.

Douay-Rheims

Let us be glad and rejoice, and give glory to him; for the marriage of the Lamb is come, and his wife hath prepared herself.

Latin (Clementine Vulgate)

Gaudeamus, et exsultemus: et demus gloriam ei: quia venerunt nuptiæ Agni, et uxor ejus præparavit se.

Arabic (Van Dyck)

لِنَفْرَحْ وَنَتَهَلَّلْ وَنُعْطِهِ ٱلْمَجْدَ! لِأَنَّ عُرْسَ ٱلْخَرُوفِ قَدْ جَاءَ، وَٱمْرَأَتُهُ هَيَّأَتْ نَفْسَهَا.

↩

Revelation 19:8.

And to her was granted that she should be arrayed in fine linen, clean and white: for the fine linen is the righteousness of saints.

Greek (Byzantine Majority Text)

Καὶ ἐδόθη αὐτῇ ἵνα περιβάληται βύσσινον λαμπρὸν καὶ καθαρόν· τὸ γὰρ βύσσινον τὰ δικαιώματα τῶν ἁγίων ἐστίν.

Douay-Rheims

And it is granted to her that she should clothe herself with fine linen, glittering and white. For the fine linen are the justifications of saints.

Latin (Clementine Vulgate)

Et datum est illi ut cooperiat se byssino splendenti et candido. Byssinum enim justificationes sunt sanctorum.

Arabic (Van Dyck)

وَأُعْطِيَتْ أَنْ تَلْبَسَ بَزًّا نَقِيًّا بَهِيًّا، لِأَنَّ ٱلْبَزَّ هُوَ تَبَرُّرَاتُ ٱلْقِدِّيسِينَ».

↩

Revelation 19:9.

And he saith unto me, Write, Blessed [are] they which are called unto the marriage supper of the Lamb. And he saith unto me, These are the true sayings of God.

Greek (Byzantine Majority Text)

Καὶ λέγει μοι, Γράψον, Μακάριοι οἱ εἰς τὸ δεῖπνον τοῦ γάμου τοῦ ἀρνίου κεκλημένοι. Καὶ λέγει μοι, Οὗτοι οἱ λόγοι ἀληθινοὶ τοῦ θεοῦ εἰσιν.

Douay-Rheims

And he said to me: Write: Blessed are they that are called to the marriage supper of the Lamb. And he saith to me: These words of God are true.

Latin (Clementine Vulgate)

Et dixit mihi: Scribe: Beati qui ad cœnam nuptiarum Agni vocati sunt; et dixit mihi: Hæc verba Dei vera sunt.

Arabic (Van Dyck)

وَقَالَ لِيَ: «ٱكْتُبْ: طُوبَى لِلْمَدْعُوِّينَ إِلَى عَشَاءِ عُرْسِ ٱلْخَرُوفِ!». وَقَالَ: «هَذِهِ هِيَ أَقْوَالُ ٱللهِ ٱلصَّادِقَةُ».

↩

Revelation 19:10.

And I fell at his feet to worship him. And he said unto me, See [thou do it] not: I am thy fellowservant, and of thy brethren that have the testimony of Jesus: worship God: for the testimony of Jesus is the spirit of prophecy.

Greek (Byzantine Majority Text)

Καὶ ἔπεσα ἔμπροσθεν τῶν ποδῶν αὐτοῦ προσκυνῆσαι αὐτῷ· καὶ λέγει μοι, Ὅρα μή· σύνδουλός σου εἰμὶ καὶ τῶν ἀδελφῶν σου τῶν ἐχόντων τὴν μαρτυρίαν Ἰησοῦ· τῷ θεῷ προσκύνησον· ἡ γὰρ μαρτυρία τοῦ Ἰησοῦ ἐστὶν τὸ πνεῦμα τῆς προφητείας.

Douay-Rheims

And I fell down before his feet, to adore him. And he saith to me: See thou do it not: I am thy fellow servant, and of thy brethren, who have the testimony of Jesus. Adore God. For the testimony of Jesus is the spirit of prophecy.

Latin (Clementine Vulgate)

Et cecidi ante pedes ejus, ut adorarem eum. Et dicit mihi: Vide ne feceris: conservus tuus sum, et fratrum tuorum habentium testimonium Jesu. Deum adora. Testimonium enim Jesu est spiritus prophetiæ.

Arabic (Van Dyck)

فَخَرَرْتُ أَمَامَ رِجْلَيْهِ لِأَسْجُدَ لَهُ، فَقَالَ لِيَ: «ٱنْظُرْ! لَا تَفْعَلْ! أَنَا عَبْدٌ مَعَكَ وَمَعَ إِخْوَتِكَ ٱلَّذِينَ عِنْدَهُمْ شَهَادَةُ يَسُوعَ. ٱسْجُدْ لِلهِ! فَإِنَّ شَهَادَةَ يَسُوعَ هِيَ رُوحُ ٱلنُّبُوَّةِ».

↩

Revelation 19:11.

And I saw heaven opened, and behold a white horse; and he that sat upon him [was] called Faithful and True, and in righteousness he doth judge and make war.

Greek (Byzantine Majority Text)

Καὶ εἶδον τὸν οὐρανὸν ἀνεῳγμένον, καὶ ἰδού, ἵππος λευκός, καὶ ὁ καθήμενος ἐπ’ αὐτόν, καλούμενος πιστὸς καὶ ἀληθινός, καὶ ἐν δικαιοσύνῃ κρίνει καὶ πολεμεῖ.

Douay-Rheims

And I saw heaven opened, and behold a white horse; and he that sat upon him was called faithful and true, and with justice doth he judge and fight.

Latin (Clementine Vulgate)

Et vidi cælum apertum, et ecce equus albus, et qui sedebat super eum, vocabatur Fidelis, et Verax, et cum justitia judicat et pugnat.

Arabic (Van Dyck)

ثُمَّ رَأَيْتُ ٱلسَّمَاءَ مَفْتُوحَةً، وَإِذَا فَرَسٌ أَبْيَضُ وَٱلْجَالِسُ عَلَيْهِ يُدْعَى أَمِينًا وَصَادِقًا، وَبِٱلْعَدْلِ يَحْكُمُ وَيُحَارِبُ.

↩

Revelation 19:12.

His eyes [were] as a flame of fire, and on his head [were] many crowns; and he had a name written, that no man knew, but he himself.

Greek (Byzantine Majority Text)

Οἱ δὲ ὀφθαλμοὶ αὐτοῦ φλὸξ πυρός, καὶ ἐπὶ τὴν κεφαλὴν αὐτοῦ διαδήματα πολλά· ἔχων ὀνόματα γεγραμμένα καὶ ὄνομα γεγραμμένον ὃ οὐδεὶς οἶδεν εἰ μὴ αὐτός,

Douay-Rheims

And his eyes were as a flame of fire, and on his head were many diadems, and he had a name written, which no man knoweth but himself.

Latin (Clementine Vulgate)

Oculi autem ejus sicut flamma ignis, et in capite ejus diademata multa, habens nomen scriptum, quod nemo novit nisi ipse.

Arabic (Van Dyck)

وَعَيْنَاهُ كَلَهِيبِ نَارٍ، وَعَلَى رَأْسِهِ تِيجَانٌ كَثِيرَةٌ، وَلَهُ ٱسْمٌ مَكْتُوبٌ لَيْسَ أَحَدٌ يَعْرِفُهُ إِلَا هُوَ.

↩

Revelation 19:13.

And he [was] clothed with a vesture dipped in blood: and his name is called The Word of God.

Greek (Byzantine Majority Text)

καὶ περιβεβλημένος ἱμάτιον βεβαμμένον αἵματι· καὶ καλεῖται τὸ ὄνομα αὐτοῦ, Ὁ λόγος τοῦ θεοῦ.

Douay-Rheims

And he was clothed with a garment sprinkled with blood; and his name is called, THE WORD OF GOD.

Latin (Clementine Vulgate)

Et vestitus erat veste aspersa sanguine: et vocatur nomen ejus: Verbum Dei.

Arabic (Van Dyck)

وَهُوَ مُتَسَرْبِلٌ بِثَوْبٍ مَغْمُوسٍ بِدَمٍ، وَيُدْعَى ٱسْمُهُ «كَلِمَةَ ٱللهِ».

↩

Revelation 19:14.

And the armies [which were] in heaven followed him upon white horses, clothed in fine linen, white and clean.

Greek (Byzantine Majority Text)

Καὶ τὰ στρατεύματα τὰ ἐν τῷ οὐρανῷ ἠκολούθει αὐτῷ ἐπὶ ἵπποις λευκοῖς, ἐνδεδυμένοι βύσσινον λευκὸν καθαρόν.

Douay-Rheims

And the armies that are in heaven followed him on white horses, clothed in fine linen, white and clean.

Latin (Clementine Vulgate)

Et exercitus qui sunt in cælo, sequebantur eum in equis albis, vestiti byssino albo et mundo.

Arabic (Van Dyck)

وَٱلْأَجْنَادُ ٱلَّذِينَ فِي ٱلسَّمَاءِ كَانُوا يَتْبَعُونَهُ عَلَى خَيْلٍ بِيضٍ، لَابِسِينَ بَزًّا أَبْيَضَ وَنَقِيًّا.

↩

Revelation 19:15.

And out of his mouth goeth a sharp sword, that with it he should smite the nations: and he shall rule them with a rod of iron: and he treadeth the winepress of the fierceness and wrath of Almighty God.

Greek (Byzantine Majority Text)

Καὶ ἐκ τοῦ στόματος αὐτοῦ ἐκπορεύεται ῥομφαία δίστομος ὀξεῖα, ἵνα ἐν αὐτῇ πατάξῃ τὰ ἔθνη· καὶ αὐτὸς ποιμανεῖ αὐτοὺς ἐν ῥάβδῳ σιδηρᾷ· καὶ αὐτὸς πατεῖ τὴν ληνὸν τοῦ οἴνου τοῦ θυμοῦ τῆς ὀργῆς τοῦ θεοῦ τοῦ παντοκράτορος.

Douay-Rheims

And out of his mouth proceedeth a sharp two edged sword; that with it he may strike the nations. And he shall rule them with a rod of iron; and he treadeth the winepress of the fierceness of the wrath of God the Almighty.

Latin (Clementine Vulgate)

Et de ore ejus procedit gladius ex utraque parte acutus, ut in ipso percutiat gentes. Et ipse reget eas in virga ferrea: et ipse calcat torcular vini furoris iræ Dei omnipotentis.

Arabic (Van Dyck)

وَمِنْ فَمِهِ يَخْرُجُ سَيْفٌ مَاضٍ لِكَيْ يَضْرِبَ بِهِ ٱلْأُمَمَ. وَهُوَ سَيَرْعَاهُمْ بِعَصًا مِنْ حَدِيدٍ، وَهُوَ يَدُوسُ مَعْصَرَةَ خَمْرِ سَخَطِ وَغَضَبِ ٱللهِ ٱلْقَادِرِ عَلَى كُلِّ شَيْءٍ.

↩

Revelation 19:16.

And he hath on [his] vesture and on his thigh a name written, KING OF KINGS, AND LORD OF LORDS.

Greek (Byzantine Majority Text)

Καὶ ἔχει ἐπὶ τὸ ἱμάτιον καὶ ἐπὶ τὸν μηρὸν αὐτοῦ ὄνομα γεγραμμένον, Βασιλεὺς βασιλέων καὶ κύριος κυρίων.

Douay-Rheims

And he hath on his garment, and on his thigh written: KING OF KINGS, AND LORD OF LORDS.

Latin (Clementine Vulgate)

Et habet in vestimento et in femore suo scriptum: Rex regum et Dominus dominantium.

Arabic (Van Dyck)

وَلَهُ عَلَى ثَوْبِهِ وَعَلَى فَخْذِهِ ٱسْمٌ مَكْتُوبٌ: «مَلِكُ ٱلْمُلُوكِ وَرَبُّ ٱلْأَرْبَابِ».

↩

Revelation 19:17.

And I saw an angel standing in the sun; and he cried with a loud voice, saying to all the fowls that fly in the midst of heaven, Come and gather yourselves together unto the supper of the great God;

Greek (Byzantine Majority Text)

Καὶ εἶδον ἄγγελον ἑστῶτα ἐν τῷ ἡλίῳ· καὶ ἔκραξεν φωνῇ μεγάλῃ, λέγων πᾶσιν τοῖς ὀρνέοις τοῖς πετομένοις ἐν μεσουρανήματι, Δεῦτε, συνάχθητε εἰς τὸ δεῖπνον τὸ μέγα τοῦ θεοῦ,

Douay-Rheims

And I saw an angel standing in the sun, and he cried with a loud voice, saying to all the birds that did fly through the midst of heaven: Come, gather yourselves together to the great supper of God:

Latin (Clementine Vulgate)

Et vidi unum angelum stantem in sole, et clamavit voce magna, dicens omnibus avibus, quæ volabant per medium cæli: Venite, et congregamini ad cœnam magnam Dei:

Arabic (Van Dyck)

وَرَأَيْتُ مَلَاكًا وَاحِدًا وَاقِفًا فِي ٱلشَّمْسِ، فَصَرَخَ بِصَوْتٍ عَظِيمٍ قَائِلًا لِجَمِيعِ ٱلطُّيُورِ ٱلطَّائِرَةِ فِي وَسَطِ ٱلسَّمَاءِ: «هَلُمَّ ٱجْتَمِعِي إِلَى عَشَاءِ ٱلْإِلَهِ ٱلْعَظِيمِ،

↩

Revelation 19:18.

That ye may eat the flesh of kings, and the flesh of captains, and the flesh of mighty men, and the flesh of horses, and of them that sit on them, and the flesh of all [men, both] free and bond, both small and great.

Greek (Byzantine Majority Text)

ἵνα φάγητε σάρκας βασιλέων, καὶ σάρκας χιλιάρχων, καὶ σάρκας ἰσχυρῶν, καὶ σάρκας ἵππων καὶ τῶν καθημένων ἐπ’ αὐτῶν, καὶ σάρκας πάντων, ἐλευθέρων τε καὶ δούλων, καὶ μικρῶν τε καὶ μεγάλων.

Douay-Rheims

That you may eat the flesh of kings, and the flesh of tribunes, and the flesh of mighty men, and the flesh of horses, and of them that sit on them, and the flesh of all freemen and bondmen, and of little and of great.

Latin (Clementine Vulgate)

ut manducetis carnes regum, et carnes tribunorum, et carnes fortium, et carnes equorum, et sedentium in ipsis, et carnes omnium liberorum, et servorum, et pusillorum et magnorum.

Arabic (Van Dyck)

لِكَيْ تَأْكُلِي لُحُومَ مُلُوكٍ، وَلُحُومَ قُوَّادٍ، وَلُحُومَ أَقْوِيَاءَ، وَلُحُومَ خَيْلٍ وَٱلْجَالِسِينَ عَلَيْهَا، وَلُحُومَ ٱلْكُلِّ: حُرًّا وَعَبْدًا، صَغِيرًا وَكَبِيرًا».

↩

Revelation 19:19.

And I saw the beast, and the kings of the earth, and their armies, gathered together to make war against him that sat on the horse, and against his army.

Greek (Byzantine Majority Text)

Καὶ εἶδον τὸ θηρίον, καὶ τοὺς βασιλεῖς τῆς γῆς, καὶ τὰ στρατεύματα αὐτῶν συνηγμένα ποιῆσαι πόλεμον μετὰ τοῦ καθημένου ἐπὶ τοῦ ἵππου, καὶ μετὰ τοῦ στρατεύματος αὐτοῦ.

Douay-Rheims

And I saw the beast, and the kings of the earth, and their armies gathered together to make war with him that sat upon the horse, and with his army.

Latin (Clementine Vulgate)

Et vidi bestiam, et reges terræ, et exercitus eorum congregatos ad faciendum prælium cum illo, qui sedebat in equo, et cum exercitu ejus.

Arabic (Van Dyck)

وَرَأَيْتُ ٱلْوَحْشَ وَمُلُوكَ ٱلْأَرْضِ وَأَجْنَادَهُمْ مُجْتَمِعِينَ لِيَصْنَعُوا حَرْبًا مَعَ ٱلْجَالِسِ عَلَى ٱلْفَرَسِ وَمَعَ جُنْدِهِ.

↩

Revelation 19:20.

And the beast was taken, and with him the false prophet that wrought miracles before him, with which he deceived them that had received the mark of the beast, and them that worshipped his image. These both were cast alive into a lake of fire burning with brimstone.

Greek (Byzantine Majority Text)

Καὶ ἐπιάσθη τὸ θηρίον, καὶ ὁ μετ’ αὐτοῦ ψευδοπροφήτης ὁ ποιήσας τὰ σημεῖα ἐνώπιον αὐτοῦ, ἐν οἷς ἐπλάνησεν τοὺς λαβόντας τὸ χάραγμα τοῦ θηρίου, καὶ τοὺς προσκυνοῦντας τῇ εἰκόνι αὐτοῦ· ζῶντες ἐβλήθησαν οἱ δύο εἰς τὴν λίμνην τοῦ πυρὸς τὴν καιομένην ἐν θείῳ·

Douay-Rheims

And the beast was taken, and with him the false prophet, who wrought signs before him, wherewith he seduced them who received the character of the beast, and who adored his image. These two were cast alive into the pool of fire, burning with brimstone.

Latin (Clementine Vulgate)

Et apprehensa est bestia, et cum ea pseudopropheta: qui fecit signa coram ipso, quibus seduxit eos, qui acceperunt caracterem bestiæ, et qui adoraverunt imaginem ejus. Vivi missi sunt hi duo in stagnum ignis ardentis sulphure:

Arabic (Van Dyck)

فَقُبِضَ عَلَى ٱلْوَحْشِ وَٱلنَّبِيِّ ٱلْكَذَّابِ مَعَهُ، ٱلصَّانِعِ قُدَّامَهُ ٱلْآيَاتِ ٱلَّتِي بِهَا أَضَلَّ ٱلَّذِينَ قَبِلُوا سِمَةَ ٱلْوَحْشِ وَٱلَّذِينَ سَجَدُوا لِصُورَتِهِ. وَطُرِحَ ٱلِٱثْنَانِ حَيَّيْنِ إِلَى بُحَيْرَةِ ٱلنَّارِ ٱلْمُتَّقِدَةِ بِٱلْكِبْرِيتِ.

↩

Revelation 19:21.

And the remnant were slain with the sword of him that sat upon the horse, which [sword] proceeded out of his mouth: and all the fowls were filled with their flesh.

Greek (Byzantine Majority Text)

καὶ οἱ λοιποὶ ἀπεκτάνθησαν ἐν τῇ ῥομφαίᾳ τοῦ καθημένου ἐπὶ τοῦ ἵππου, τῇ ἐξελθούσῃ ἐκ τοῦ στόματος αὐτοῦ· καὶ πάντα τὰ ὄρνεα ἐχορτάσθησαν ἐκ τῶν σαρκῶν αὐτῶν.

Douay-Rheims

And the rest were slain by the sword of him that sitteth upon the horse, which proceedeth out of his mouth; and all the birds were filled with their flesh.

Latin (Clementine Vulgate)

et ceteri occisi sunt in gladio sedentis super equum, qui procedit de ore ipsius: et omnes aves saturatæ sunt carnibus eorum.

Arabic (Van Dyck)

وَٱلْبَاقُونَ قُتِلُوا بِسَيْفِ ٱلْجَالِسِ عَلَى ٱلْفَرَسِ ٱلْخَارِجِ مِنْ فَمِهِ، وَجَمِيعُ ٱلطُّيُورِ شَبِعَتْ مِنْ لُحُومِهِمْ.

↩

Revelation 20:1.

And I saw an angel come down from heaven, having the key of the bottomless pit and a great chain in his hand.

Greek (Byzantine Majority Text)

Καὶ εἶδον ἄγγελον καταβαίνοντα ἐκ τοῦ οὐρανοῦ, ἔχοντα τὴν κλεῖν τῆς ἀβύσσου, καὶ ἅλυσιν μεγάλην ἐπὶ τὴν χεῖρα αὐτοῦ.

Douay-Rheims

And I saw an angel coming down from heaven, having the key of the bottomless pit, and a great chain in his hand.

Latin (Clementine Vulgate)

Et vidi angelum descendentem de cælo, habentem clavem abyssi, et catenam magnam in manu sua.

Arabic (Van Dyck)

وَرَأَيْتُ مَلَاكًا نَازِلًا مِنَ ٱلسَّمَاءِ مَعَهُ مِفْتَاحُ ٱلْهَاوِيَةِ، وَسِلْسِلَةٌ عَظِيمَةٌ عَلَى يَدِهِ.

↩

Revelation 20:2.

And he laid hold on the dragon, that old serpent, which is the Devil, and Satan, and bound him a thousand years,

Greek (Byzantine Majority Text)

Καὶ ἐκράτησεν τὸν δράκοντα, τὸν ὄφιν τὸν ἀρχαῖον, ὅς ἐστιν διάβολος καὶ ὁ Σατανᾶς, ὁ πλανῶν τὴν οἰκουμένην ὅλην, καὶ ἔδησεν αὐτὸν χίλια ἔτη,

Douay-Rheims

And he laid hold on the dragon the old serpent, which is the devil and Satan, and bound him for a thousand years.

Latin (Clementine Vulgate)

Et apprehendit draconem, serpentem antiquum, qui est diabolus, et Satanas, et ligavit eum per annos mille:

Arabic (Van Dyck)

فَقَبَضَ عَلَى ٱلتِّنِّينِ، ٱلْحَيَّةِ ٱلْقَدِيمَةِ، ٱلَّذِي هُوَ إِبْلِيسُ وَٱلشَّيْطَانُ، وَقَيَّدَهُ أَلْفَ سَنَةٍ،

↩

Revelation 20:3.

And cast him into the bottomless pit, and shut him up, and set a seal upon him, that he should deceive the nations no more, till the thousand years should be fulfilled: and after that he must be loosed a little season.

Greek (Byzantine Majority Text)

καὶ ἔβαλεν αὐτὸν εἰς τὴν ἄβυσσον, καὶ ἔκλεισεν καὶ ἐσφράγισεν ἐπάνω αὐτοῦ, ἵνα μὴ πλανᾷ ἔτι τὰ ἔθνη, ἄχρι τελεσθῇ τὰ χίλια ἔτη· καὶ μετὰ ταῦτα δεῖ αὐτὸν λυθῆναι μικρὸν χρόνον.

Douay-Rheims

And he cast him into the bottomless pit, and shut him up, and set a seal upon him, that he should no more seduce the nations, till the thousand years be finished. And after that, he must be loosed a little time.

Latin (Clementine Vulgate)

et misit eum in abyssum, et clausit, et signavit super illum ut non seducat amplius gentes, donec consummentur mille anni: et post hæc oportet illum solvi modico tempore.

Arabic (Van Dyck)

وَطَرَحَهُ فِي ٱلْهَاوِيَةِ وَأَغْلَقَ عَلَيْهِ، وَخَتَمَ عَلَيْهِ لِكَيْ لَا يُضِلَّ ٱلْأُمَمَ فِي مَا بَعْدُ، حَتَّى تَتِمَّ ٱلْأَلْفُ ٱلسَّنَةِ. وَبَعْدَ ذَلِكَ لَابُدَّ أَنْ يُحَلَّ زَمَانًا يَسِيرًا.

↩

Revelation 20:4.

And I saw thrones, and they sat upon them, and judgment was given unto them: and [I saw] the souls of them that were beheaded for the witness of Jesus, and for the word of God, and which had not worshipped the beast, neither his image, neither had received [his] mark upon their foreheads, or in their hands; and they lived and reigned with Christ a thousand years.

Greek (Byzantine Majority Text)

Καὶ εἶδον θρόνους, καὶ ἐκάθισαν ἐπ’ αὐτούς, καὶ κρίμα ἐδόθη αὐτοῖς· καὶ τὰς ψυχὰς τῶν πεπελεκισμένων διὰ τὴν μαρτυρίαν Ἰησοῦ, καὶ διὰ τὸν λόγον τοῦ θεοῦ, καὶ οἵτινες οὐ προσεκύνησαν τὸ θηρίον, οὐδὲ τὴν εἰκόνα αὐτοῦ, καὶ οὐκ ἔλαβον τὸ χάραγμα ἐπὶ τὸ μέτωπον, καὶ ἐπὶ τὴν χεῖρα αὐτῶν· καὶ ἔζησαν, καὶ ἐβασίλευσαν μετὰ τοῦ χριστοῦ τὰ χίλια ἔτη.

Douay-Rheims

And I saw seats; and they sat upon them; and judgment was given unto them; and the souls of them that were beheaded for the testimony of Jesus, and for the word of God, and who had not adored the beast nor his image, nor received his character on their foreheads, or in their hands; and they lived and reigned with Christ a thousand years.

Latin (Clementine Vulgate)

Et vidi sedes, et sederunt super eas, et judicium datum est illis: et animas decollatorum propter testimonium Jesu, et propter verbum Dei, et qui non adoraverunt bestiam, neque imaginem ejus, nec acceperunt caracterem ejus in frontibus, aut in manibus suis, et vixerunt, et regnaverunt cum Christo mille annis.

Arabic (Van Dyck)

وَرَأَيْتُ عُرُوشًا فَجَلَسُوا عَلَيْهَا، وَأُعْطُوا حُكْمًا. وَرَأَيْتُ نُفُوسَ ٱلَّذِينَ قُتِلُوا مِنْ أَجْلِ شَهَادَةِ يَسُوعَ وَمِنْ أَجْلِ كَلِمَةِ ٱللهِ، وَٱلَّذِينَ لَمْ يَسْجُدُوا لِلْوَحْشِ وَلَا لِصُورَتِهِ، وَلَمْ يَقْبَلُوا ٱلسِّمَةَ عَلَى جِبَاهِهِمْ وَعَلَى أَيْدِيهِمْ، فَعَاشُوا وَمَلَكُوا مَعَ ٱلْمَسِيحِ أَلْفَ سَنَةٍ.

↩

Revelation 20:5.

But the rest of the dead lived not again until the thousand years were finished. This [is] the first resurrection.

Greek (Byzantine Majority Text)

Καὶ οἱ λοιποὶ τῶν νεκρῶν οὐκ ἔζησαν ἄχρι τελεσθῇ τὰ χίλια ἔτη. Αὕτη ἡ ἀνάστασις ἡ πρώτη.

Douay-Rheims

The rest of the dead lived not, till the thousand years were finished. This is the first resurrection.

Latin (Clementine Vulgate)

Ceteri mortuorum non vixerunt, donec consummentur mille anni. Hæc est resurrectio prima.

Arabic (Van Dyck)

وَأَمَّا بَقِيَّةُ ٱلْأَمْوَاتِ فَلَمْ تَعِشْ حَتَّى تَتِمَّ ٱلْأَلْفُ ٱلسَّنَةِ. هَذِهِ هِيَ ٱلْقِيَامَةُ ٱلْأُولَى.

↩

Revelation 20:6.

Blessed and holy [is] he that hath part in the first resurrection: on such the second death hath no power, but they shall be priests of God and of Christ, and shall reign with him a thousand years.

Greek (Byzantine Majority Text)

Μακάριος καὶ ἅγιος ὁ ἔχων μέρος ἐν τῇ ἀναστάσει τῇ πρώτῃ· ἐπὶ τούτων ὁ δεύτερος θάνατος οὐκ ἔχει ἐξουσίαν, ἀλλ’ ἔσονται ἱερεῖς τοῦ θεοῦ καὶ τοῦ χριστοῦ, καὶ βασιλεύσουσιν μετ’ αὐτοῦ χίλια ἔτη.

Douay-Rheims

Blessed and holy is he that hath part in the first resurrection. In these the second death hath no power; but they shall be priests of God and of Christ; and shall reign with him a thousand years.

Latin (Clementine Vulgate)

Beatus, et sanctus, qui habet partem in resurrectione prima: in his secunda mors non habet potestatem: sed erunt sacerdotes Dei et Christi, et regnabunt cum illo mille annis.

Arabic (Van Dyck)

مُبَارَكٌ وَمُقَدَّسٌ مَنْ لَهُ نَصِيبٌ فِي ٱلْقِيَامَةِ ٱلْأُولَى. هَؤُلَاءِ لَيْسَ لِلْمَوْتِ ٱلثَّانِي سُلْطَانٌ عَلَيْهِمْ، بَلْ سَيَكُونُونَ كَهَنَةً لِلهِ وَٱلْمَسِيحِ، وَسَيَمْلِكُونَ مَعَهُ أَلْفَ سَنَةٍ.

↩

Revelation 20:7.

And when the thousand years are expired, Satan shall be loosed out of his prison,

Greek (Byzantine Majority Text)

Καὶ ὅταν τελεσθῇ τὰ χίλια ἔτη, λυθήσεται ὁ Σατανᾶς ἐκ τῆς φυλακῆς αὐτοῦ,

Arabic (Van Dyck)

ثُمَّ مَتَى تَمَّتِ ٱلْأَلْفُ ٱلسَّنَةِ يُحَلُّ ٱلشَّيْطَانُ مِنْ سِجْنِهِ،

↩

Revelation 20:8.

And shall go out to deceive the nations which are in the four quarters of the earth, Gog and Magog, to gather them together to battle: the number of whom [is] as the sand of the sea.

Greek (Byzantine Majority Text)

καὶ ἐξελεύσεται πλανῆσαι τὰ ἔθνη τὰ ἐν ταῖς τέσσαρσιν γωνίαις τῆς γῆς, τὸν Γὼγ καὶ τὸν Μαγώγ, συναγαγεῖν αὐτοὺς εἰς τὸν πόλεμον· ὧν ὁ ἀριθμὸς ὡς ἡ ἄμμος τῆς θαλάσσης.

Douay-Rheims

And when the thousand years shall be finished, Satan shall be loosed out of his prison, and shall go forth, and seduce the nations, which are over the four quarters of the earth, Gog, and Magog, and shall gather them together to battle, the number of whom is as the sand of the sea.

Latin (Clementine Vulgate)

Et cum consummati fuerint mille anni, solvetur Satanas de carcere suo, et exibit, et seducet gentes, quæ sunt super quatuor angulos terræ, Gog, et Magog, et congregabit eos in prælium, quorum numerus est sicut arena maris.

Arabic (Van Dyck)

وَيَخْرُجُ لِيُضِلَّ ٱلْأُمَمَ ٱلَّذِينَ فِي أَرْبَعِ زَوَايَا ٱلْأَرْضِ: جُوجَ وَمَاجُوجَ، لِيَجْمَعَهُمْ لِلْحَرْبِ، ٱلَّذِينَ عَدَدُهُمْ مِثْلُ رَمْلِ ٱلْبَحْرِ.

↩

Revelation 20:9.

And they went up on the breadth of the earth, and compassed the camp of the saints about, and the beloved city: and fire came down from God out of heaven, and devoured them.

Greek (Byzantine Majority Text)

Καὶ ἀνέβησαν ἐπὶ τὸ πλάτος τῆς γῆς, καὶ ἐκύκλωσαν τὴν παρεμβολὴν τῶν ἁγίων καὶ τὴν πόλιν τὴν ἠγαπημένην· καὶ κατέβη πῦρ ἐκ τοῦ οὐρανοῦ ἀπὸ τοῦ θεοῦ, καὶ κατέφαγεν αὐτούς.

Douay-Rheims

And they came upon the breadth of the earth, and encompassed the camp of the saints, and the beloved city.

Latin (Clementine Vulgate)

Et ascenderunt super latitudinem terræ, et circuierunt castra sanctorum, et civitatem dilectam.

Arabic (Van Dyck)

فَصَعِدُوا عَلَى عَرْضِ ٱلْأَرْضِ، وَأَحَاطُوا بِمُعَسْكَرِ ٱلْقِدِّيسِينَ وَبِٱلْمَدِينَةِ ٱلْمَحْبُوبَةِ، فَنَزَلَتْ نَارٌ مِنْ عِنْدِ ٱللهِ مِنَ ٱلسَّمَاءِ وَأَكَلَتْهُمْ.

↩

Revelation 20:10.

And the devil that deceived them was cast into the lake of fire and brimstone, where the beast and the false prophet [are], and shall be tormented day and night for ever and ever.

Greek (Byzantine Majority Text)

Καὶ ὁ διάβολος ὁ πλανῶν αὐτοὺς ἐβλήθη εἰς τὴν λίμνην τοῦ πυρὸς καὶ θείου, ὅπου καὶ τὸ θηρίον καὶ ὁ ψευδοπροφήτης· καὶ βασανισθήσονται ἡμέρας καὶ νυκτὸς εἰς τοὺς αἰῶνας τῶν αἰώνων.

Douay-Rheims

And there came down fire from God out of heaven, and devoured them; and the devil, who seduced them, was cast into the pool of fire and brimstone, where both the beast And the false prophet shall be tormented day and night for ever and ever.

Latin (Clementine Vulgate)

Et descendit ignis a Deo de cælo, et devoravit eos: et diabolus, qui seducebat eos, missus est in stagnum ignis, et sulphuris, ubi et bestia et pseudopropheta cruciabuntur die ac nocte in sæcula sæculorum.

Arabic (Van Dyck)

وَإِبْلِيسُ ٱلَّذِي كَانَ يُضِلُّهُمْ طُرِحَ فِي بُحَيْرَةِ ٱلنَّارِ وَٱلْكِبْرِيتِ، حَيْثُ ٱلْوَحْشُ وَٱلنَّبِيُّ ٱلْكَذَّابُ. وَسَيُعَذَّبُونَ نَهَارًا وَلَيْلًا إِلَى أَبَدِ ٱلْآبِدِينَ.

↩

Revelation 20:11.

And I saw a great white throne, and him that sat on it, from whose face the earth and the heaven fled away; and there was found no place for them.

Greek (Byzantine Majority Text)

Καὶ εἶδον θρόνον μέγαν λευκόν, καὶ τὸν καθήμενον ἐπ’ αὐτόν, οὗ ἀπὸ προσώπου ἔφυγεν ἡ γῆ καὶ ὁ οὐρανός, καὶ τόπος οὐχ εὑρέθη αὐτοῖς.

Douay-Rheims

And I saw a great white throne, and one sitting upon it, from whose face the earth and heaven fled away, and there was no place found for them.

Latin (Clementine Vulgate)

Et vidi thronum magnum candidum, et sedentem super eum, a cujus conspectu fugit terra, et cælum, et locus non est inventus eis.

Arabic (Van Dyck)

ثُمَّ رَأَيْتُ عَرْشًا عَظِيمًا أَبْيَضَ، وَٱلْجَالِسَ عَلَيْهِ، ٱلَّذِي مِنْ وَجْهِهِ هَرَبَتِ ٱلْأَرْضُ وَٱلسَّمَاءُ، وَلَمْ يُوجَدْ لَهُمَا مَوْضِعٌ!

↩

Revelation 20:12.

And I saw the dead, small and great, stand before God; and the books were opened: and another book was opened, which is [the book] of life: and the dead were judged out of those things which were written in the books, according to their works.

Greek (Byzantine Majority Text)

Καὶ εἶδον τοὺς νεκρούς, τοὺς μεγάλους καὶ τοὺς μικρούς, ἑστῶτας ἐνώπιον τοῦ θρόνου, καὶ βιβλία ἠνεῴχθησαν· καὶ ἄλλο βιβλίον ἠνεῴχθη, ὅ ἐστιν τῆς ζωῆς· καὶ ἐκρίθησαν οἱ νεκροὶ ἐκ τῶν γεγραμμένων ἐν τοῖς βιβλίοις, κατὰ τὰ ἔργα αὐτῶν.

Douay-Rheims

And I saw the dead, great and small, standing in the presence of the throne, and the books were opened; and another book was opened, which is the book of life; and the dead were judged by those things which were written in the books, according to their works.

Latin (Clementine Vulgate)

Et vidi mortuos, magnos et pusillos, stantes in conspectu throni, et libri aperti sunt: et alius liber apertus est, qui est vitæ: et judicati sunt mortui ex his, quæ scripta erant in libris, secundum opera ipsorum:

Arabic (Van Dyck)

وَرَأَيْتُ ٱلْأَمْوَاتَ صِغَارًا وَكِبَارًا وَاقِفِينَ أَمَامَ ٱللهِ، وَٱنْفَتَحَتْ أَسْفَارٌ، وَٱنْفَتَحَ سِفْرٌ آخَرُ هُوَ سِفْرُ ٱلْحَيَاةِ، وَدِينَ ٱلْأَمْوَاتُ مِمَّا هُوَ مَكْتُوبٌ فِي ٱلْأَسْفَارِ بِحَسَبِ أَعْمَالِهِمْ.

↩

Revelation 20:13.

And the sea gave up the dead which were in it; and death and hell delivered up the dead which were in them: and they were judged every man according to their works.

Greek (Byzantine Majority Text)

Καὶ ἔδωκεν ἡ θάλασσα τοὺς νεκροὺς τοὺς ἐν αὐτῇ, καὶ ὁ Θάνατος καὶ ὁ ᾍδης ἔδωκαν τοὺς νεκροὺς τοὺς ἐν αὐτοῖς· καὶ ἐκρίθησαν ἕκαστος κατὰ τὰ ἔργα αὐτῶν.

Douay-Rheims

And the sea gave up the dead that were in it, and death and hell gave up their dead that were in them; and they were judged every one according to their works.

Latin (Clementine Vulgate)

et dedit mare mortuos, qui in eo erant: et mors et infernus dederunt mortuos suos, qui in ipsis erant: et judicatum est de singulis secundum opera ipsorum.

Arabic (Van Dyck)

وَسَلَّمَ ٱلْبَحْرُ ٱلْأَمْوَاتَ ٱلَّذِينَ فِيهِ، وَسَلَّمَ ٱلْمَوْتُ وَٱلْهَاوِيَةُ ٱلْأَمْوَاتَ ٱلَّذِينَ فِيهِمَا. وَدِينُوا كُلُّ وَاحِدٍ بِحَسَبِ أَعْمَالِهِ.

↩

Revelation 20:14.

And death and hell were cast into the lake of fire. This is the second death.

Greek (Byzantine Majority Text)

Καὶ ὁ Θάνατος καὶ ὁ ᾍδης ἐβλήθησαν εἰς τὴν λίμνην τοῦ πυρός· οὗτος ὁ θάνατος ὁ δεύτερός ἐστιν, ἡ λίμνη τοῦ πυρός.

Douay-Rheims

And hell and death were cast into the pool of fire. This is the second death.

Latin (Clementine Vulgate)

Et infernus et mors missi sunt in stagnum ignis. Hæc est mors secunda.

Arabic (Van Dyck)

وَطُرِحَ ٱلْمَوْتُ وَٱلْهَاوِيَةُ فِي بُحَيْرَةِ ٱلنَّارِ. هَذَا هُوَ ٱلْمَوْتُ ٱلثَّانِي.

↩

Revelation 20:15.

And whosoever was not found written in the book of life was cast into the lake of fire.

Greek (Byzantine Majority Text)

Καὶ εἴ τις οὐχ εὑρέθη ἐν τῷ βιβλίῳ τῆς ζωῆς γεγραμμένος, ἐβλήθη εἰς τὴν λίμνην τοῦ πυρός.

Douay-Rheims

And whosoever was not found written in the book of life, was cast into the pool of fire.

Latin (Clementine Vulgate)

Et qui non inventus est in libro vitæ scriptus, missus est in stagnum ignis.

Arabic (Van Dyck)

وَكُلُّ مَنْ لَمْ يُوجَدْ مَكْتُوبًا فِي سِفْرِ ٱلْحَيَاةِ طُرِحَ فِي بُحَيْرَةِ ٱلنَّارِ.

↩

Revelation 21:1.

And I saw a new heaven and a new earth: for the first heaven and the first earth were passed away; and there was no more sea.

Greek (Byzantine Majority Text)

Καὶ εἶδον οὐρανὸν καινὸν καὶ γῆν καινήν· ὁ γὰρ πρῶτος οὐρανὸς καὶ ἡ πρώτη γῆ ἀπῆλθον, καὶ ἡ θάλασσα οὐκ ἔστιν ἔτι.

Douay-Rheims

And I saw a new heaven and a new earth. For the first heaven and the first earth was gone, and the sea is now no more.

Latin (Clementine Vulgate)

Et vidi cælum novum et terram novam. Primum enim cælum, et prima terra abiit, et mare jam non est.

Arabic (Van Dyck)

ثُمَّ رَأَيْتُ سَمَاءً جَدِيدَةً وَأَرْضًا جَدِيدَةً، لِأَنَّ ٱلسَّمَاءَ ٱلْأُولَى وَٱلْأَرْضَ ٱلْأُولَى مَضَتَا، وَٱلْبَحْرُ لَا يُوجَدُ فِي مَا بَعْدُ.

↩

Revelation 21:2.

And I John saw the holy city, new Jerusalem, coming down from God out of heaven, prepared as a bride adorned for her husband.

Greek (Byzantine Majority Text)

Καὶ τὴν πόλιν τὴν ἁγίαν, Ἱερουσαλὴμ καινήν, εἶδον καταβαίνουσαν ἐκ τοῦ οὐρανοῦ ἀπὸ τοῦ θεοῦ, ἡτοιμασμένην ὡς νύμφην κεκοσμημένην τῷ ἀνδρὶ αὐτῆς.

Douay-Rheims

And I John saw the holy city, the new Jerusalem, coming down out of heaven from God, prepared as a bride adorned for her husband.

Latin (Clementine Vulgate)

Et ego Joannes vidi sanctam civitatem Jerusalem novam descendentem de cælo a Deo, paratam sicut sponsam ornatam viro suo.

Arabic (Van Dyck)

وَأَنَا يُوحَنَّا رَأَيْتُ ٱلْمَدِينَةَ ٱلْمُقَدَّسَةَ أُورُشَلِيمَ ٱلْجَدِيدَةَ نَازِلَةً مِنَ ٱلسَّمَاءِ مِنْ عِنْدِ ٱللهِ مُهَيَّأَةً كَعَرُوسٍ مُزَيَّنَةٍ لِرَجُلِهَا.

↩

Revelation 21:3.

And I heard a great voice out of heaven saying, Behold, the tabernacle of God [is] with men, and he will dwell with them, and they shall be his people, and God himself shall be with them, [and be] their God.

Greek (Byzantine Majority Text)

Καὶ ἤκουσα φωνῆς μεγάλης ἐκ τοῦ οὐρανοῦ, λεγούσης, Ἰδού, ἡ σκηνὴ τοῦ θεοῦ μετὰ τῶν ἀνθρώπων, καὶ σκηνώσει μετ’ αὐτῶν, καὶ αὐτοὶ λαὸς αὐτοῦ ἔσονται, καὶ αὐτὸς ὁ θεὸς ἔσται μετ’ αὐτῶν·

Douay-Rheims

And I heard a great voice from the throne, saying: Behold the tabernacle of God with men, and he will dwell with them. And they shall be his people; and God himself with them shall be their God.

Latin (Clementine Vulgate)

Et audivi vocem magnam de throno dicentem: Ecce tabernaculum Dei cum hominibus, et habitabit cum eis. Et ipsi populus ejus erunt, et ipse Deus cum eis erit eorum Deus:

Arabic (Van Dyck)

وَسَمِعْتُ صَوْتًا عَظِيمًا مِنَ ٱلسَّمَاءِ قَائِلًا: «هُوَذَا مَسْكَنُ ٱللهِ مَعَ ٱلنَّاسِ، وَهُوَ سَيَسْكُنُ مَعَهُمْ، وَهُمْ يَكُونُونَ لَهُ شَعْبًا، وَٱللهُ نَفْسُهُ يَكُونُ مَعَهُمْ إِلَهًا لَهُمْ.

↩

Revelation 21:4.

And God shall wipe away all tears from their eyes; and there shall be no more death, neither sorrow, nor crying, neither shall there be any more pain: for the former things are passed away.

Greek (Byzantine Majority Text)

καὶ ἐξαλείψει πᾶν δάκρυον ἀπὸ τῶν ὀφθαλμῶν αὐτῶν, καὶ ὁ θάνατος οὐκ ἔσται ἔτι· οὔτε πένθος, οὔτε κραυγή, οὔτε πόνος οὐκ ἔσται ἔτι· ὅτι τὰ πρῶτα ἀπῆλθον.

Douay-Rheims

And God shall wipe away all tears from their eyes: and death shall be no more, nor mourning, nor crying, nor sorrow shall be any more, for the former things are passed away.

Latin (Clementine Vulgate)

et absterget Deus omnem lacrimam ab oculis eorum: et mors ultra non erit, neque luctus, neque clamor, neque dolor erit ultra, quia prima abierunt.

Arabic (Van Dyck)

وَسَيَمْسَحُ ٱللهُ كُلَّ دَمْعَةٍ مِنْ عُيُونِهِمْ، وَٱلْمَوْتُ لَا يَكُونُ فِي مَا بَعْدُ، وَلَا يَكُونُ حُزْنٌ وَلَا صُرَاخٌ وَلَا وَجَعٌ فِي مَا بَعْدُ، لِأَنَّ ٱلْأُمُورَ ٱلْأُولَى قَدْ مَضَتْ».

↩

Revelation 21:5.

And he that sat upon the throne said, Behold, I make all things new. And he said unto me, Write: for these words are true and faithful.

Greek (Byzantine Majority Text)

Καὶ εἶπεν ὁ καθήμενος ἐπὶ τῷ θρόνῳ, Ἰδού, πάντα καινὰ ποιῶ. Καὶ λέγει μοι, Γράψον· ὅτι οὗτοι οἱ λόγοι ἀληθινοὶ καὶ πιστοί εἰσιν.

Douay-Rheims

And he that sat on the throne, said: Behold, I make all things new. And he said to me: Write, for these words are most faithful and true.

Latin (Clementine Vulgate)

Et dixit qui sedebat in throno: Ecce nova facio omnia. Et dixit mihi: Scribe, quia hæc verba fidelissima sunt, et vera.

Arabic (Van Dyck)

وَقَالَ ٱلْجَالِسُ عَلَى ٱلْعَرْشِ: «هَا أَنَا أَصْنَعُ كُلَّ شَيْءٍ جَدِيدًا!». وَقَالَ لِيَ: «ٱكْتُبْ: فَإِنَّ هَذِهِ ٱلْأَقْوَالَ صَادِقَةٌ وَأَمِينَةٌ».

↩

Revelation 21:6.

And he said unto me, It is done. I am Alpha and Omega, the beginning and the end. I will give unto him that is athirst of the fountain of the water of life freely.

Greek (Byzantine Majority Text)

Καὶ εἶπέν μοι, Γέγονα τὸ Ἄλφα καὶ τὸ Ὦ, ἡ ἀρχὴ καὶ τὸ τέλος. Ἐγὼ τῷ διψῶντι δώσω ἐκ τῆς πηγῆς τοῦ ὕδατος τῆς ζωῆς δωρεάν.

Douay-Rheims

And he said to me: It is done. I am Alpha and Omega; the beginning and the end. To him that thirsteth, I will give of the fountain of the water of life, freely.

Latin (Clementine Vulgate)

Et dixit mihi: Factum est: ego sum alpha et omega, initium et finis. Ego sitienti dabo de fonte aquæ vitæ, gratis.

Arabic (Van Dyck)

ثُمَّ قَالَ لِي: «قَدْ تَمَّ! أَنَا هُوَ ٱلْأَلِفُ وَٱلْيَاءُ، ٱلْبِدَايَةُ وَٱلنِّهَايَةُ. أَنَا أُعْطِي ٱلْعَطْشَانَ مِنْ يَنْبُوعِ مَاءِ ٱلْحَيَاةِ مَجَّانًا.

↩

Revelation 21:7.

He that overcometh shall inherit all things; and I will be his God, and he shall be my son.

Greek (Byzantine Majority Text)

Ὁ νικῶν κληρονομήσει ταῦτα, καὶ ἔσομαι αὐτῷ θεός, καὶ αὐτὸς ἔσται μοι υἱός.

Douay-Rheims

He that shall overcome shall possess these things, and I will be his God; and he shall be my son.

Latin (Clementine Vulgate)

Qui vicerit, possidebit hæc: et ero illi Deus, et ille erit mihi filius.

Arabic (Van Dyck)

مَنْ يَغْلِبْ يَرِثْ كُلَّ شَيْءٍ، وَأَكُونُ لَهُ إِلَهًا وَهُوَ يَكُونُ لِيَ ٱبْنًا.

↩

Revelation 21:8.

But the fearful, and unbelieving, and the abominable, and murderers, and whoremongers, and sorcerers, and idolaters, and all liars, shall have their part in the lake which burneth with fire and brimstone: which is the second death.

Greek (Byzantine Majority Text)

Τοῖς δὲ δειλοῖς καὶ ἀπίστοις καὶ ἁμαρτωλοῖς καὶ ἐβδελυγμένοις καὶ φονεῦσιν καὶ πόρνοις καὶ φαρμάκοις καὶ εἰδωλολάτραις, καὶ πᾶσιν τοῖς ψευδέσιν, τὸ μέρος αὐτῶν ἐν τῇ λίμνῃ τῇ καιομένῃ πυρὶ καὶ θείῳ, ὅ ἐστιν ὁ θάνατος ὁ δεύτερος.

Douay-Rheims

But the fearful, and unbelieving, and the abominable, and murderers, and whoremongers, and sorcerers, and idolaters, and all liars, they shall have their portion in the pool burning with fire and brimstone, which is the second death.

Latin (Clementine Vulgate)

Timidis autem, et incredulis, et execratis, et homicidis, et fornicatoribus, et veneficis, et idolatris, et omnibus mendacibus, pars illorum erit in stagno ardenti igne et sulphure: quod est mors secunda.

Arabic (Van Dyck)

وَأَمَّا ٱلْخَائِفُونَ وَغَيْرُ ٱلْمُؤْمِنِينَ وَٱلرَّجِسُونَ وَٱلْقَاتِلُونَ وَٱلزُّنَاةُ وَٱلسَّحَرَةُ وَعَبَدَةُ ٱلْأَوْثَانِ وَجَمِيعُ ٱلْكَذَبَةِ، فَنَصِيبُهُمْ فِي ٱلْبُحَيْرَةِ ٱلْمُتَّقِدَةِ بِنَارٍ وَكِبْرِيتٍ، ٱلَّذِي هُوَ ٱلْمَوْتُ ٱلثَّانِي».

↩

Revelation 21:9.

And there came unto me one of the seven angels which had the seven vials full of the seven last plagues, and talked with me, saying, Come hither, I will shew thee the bride, the Lamb’s wife.

Greek (Byzantine Majority Text)

Καὶ ἦλθεν εἷς ἐκ τῶν ἑπτὰ ἀγγέλων τῶν ἐχόντων τὰς ἑπτὰ φιάλας γεμούσας τῶν ἑπτὰ πληγῶν τῶν ἐσχάτων, καὶ ἐλάλησεν μετ’ ἐμοῦ, λέγων, Δεῦρο, δείξω σοι τὴν γυναῖκα τὴν νύμφην τοῦ ἀρνίου.

Douay-Rheims

And there came one of the seven angels, who had the vials full of the seven last plagues, and spoke with me, saying: Come, and I will shew thee the bride, the wife of the Lamb.

Latin (Clementine Vulgate)

Et venit unus de septem angelis habentibus phialas plenas septem plagis novissimis, et locutus est mecum, dicens: Veni, et ostendam tibi sponsam, uxorem Agni.

Arabic (Van Dyck)

ثُمَّ جَاءَ إِلَيَّ وَاحِدٌ مِنَ ٱلسَّبْعَةِ ٱلْمَلَائِكَةِ ٱلَّذِينَ مَعَهُمُ ٱلسَّبْعَةُ ٱلْجَامَاتِ ٱلْمَمْلُوَّةِ مِنَ ٱلسَّبْعِ ٱلضَّرَبَاتِ ٱلْأَخِيرَةِ، وَتَكَلَّمَ مَعِي قَائِلًا: «هَلُمَّ فَأُرِيَكَ ٱلْعَرُوسَ ٱمْرَأَةَ ٱلْخَرُوفِ».

↩

Revelation 21:10.

And he carried me away in the spirit to a great and high mountain, and shewed me that great city, the holy Jerusalem, descending out of heaven from God,

Greek (Byzantine Majority Text)

Καὶ ἀπήνεγκέν με ἐν πνεύματι ἐπ’ ὄρος μέγα καὶ ὑψηλόν, καὶ ἔδειξέν μοι τὴν πόλιν τὴν μεγάλην, τὴν ἁγίαν Ἱερουσαλήμ, καταβαίνουσαν ἐκ τοῦ οὐρανοῦ ἀπὸ τοῦ θεοῦ,

Douay-Rheims

And he took me up in spirit to a great and high mountain: and he shewed me the holy city Jerusalem coming down out of heaven from God,

Latin (Clementine Vulgate)

Et sustulit me in spiritu in montem magnum et altum, et ostendit mihi civitatem sanctam Jerusalem descendentem de cælo a Deo,

Arabic (Van Dyck)

وَذَهَبَ بِي بِٱلرُّوحِ إِلَى جَبَلٍ عَظِيمٍ عَالٍ، وَأَرَانِي ٱلْمَدِينَةَ ٱلْعَظِيمَةَ أُورُشَلِيمَ ٱلْمُقَدَّسَةَ نَازِلَةً مِنَ ٱلسَّمَاءِ مِنْ عِنْدِ ٱللهِ،

↩

Revelation 21:11.

Having the glory of God: and her light [was] like unto a stone most precious, even like a jasper stone, clear as crystal;

Greek (Byzantine Majority Text)

ἔχουσαν τὴν δόξαν τοῦ θεοῦ· ὁ φωστὴρ αὐτῆς ὅμοιος λίθῳ τιμιωτάτῳ, ὡς λίθῳ ἰάσπιδι κρυσταλλίζοντι·

Douay-Rheims

Having the glory of God, and the light thereof was like to a precious stone, as to the jasper stone, even as crystal.

Latin (Clementine Vulgate)

habentem claritatem Dei: et lumen ejus simile lapidi pretioso tamquam lapidi jaspidis, sicut crystallum.

Arabic (Van Dyck)

لَهَا مَجْدُ ٱللهِ، وَلَمَعَانُهَا شِبْهُ أَكْرَمِ حَجَرٍ كَحَجَرِ يَشْبٍ بَلُّورِيٍّ.

↩

Revelation 21:12.

And had a wall great and high, [and] had twelve gates, and at the gates twelve angels, and names written thereon, which are [the names] of the twelve tribes of the children of Israel:

Greek (Byzantine Majority Text)

ἔχουσα τεῖχος μέγα καὶ ὑψηλόν, ἔχουσα πυλῶνας δώδεκα, καὶ ἐπὶ τοῖς πυλῶσιν ἀγγέλους δώδεκα, καὶ ὀνόματα ἐπιγεγραμμένα, ἅ ἐστιν ὀνόματα τῶν δώδεκα φυλῶν τῶν υἱῶν Ἰσραήλ.

Douay-Rheims

And it had a wall great and high, having twelve gates, and in the gates twelve angels, and names written thereon, which are the names of the twelve tribes of the children of Israel.

Latin (Clementine Vulgate)

Et habebat murum magnum, et altum, habentem portas duodecim: et in portis angelos duodecim, et nomina inscripta, quæ sunt nomina duodecim tribuum filiorum Israël:

Arabic (Van Dyck)

وَكَانَ لَهَا سُورٌ عَظِيمٌ وَعَالٍ، وَكَانَ لَهَا ٱثْنَا عَشَرَ بَابًا، وَعَلَى ٱلْأَبْوَابِ ٱثْنَا عَشَرَ مَلَاكًا، وَأَسْمَاءٌ مَكْتُوبَةٌ هِيَ أَسْمَاءُ أَسْبَاطِ بَنِي إِسْرَائِيلَ ٱلِٱثْنَيْ عَشَرَ.

↩

Revelation 21:13.

On the east three gates; on the north three gates; on the south three gates; and on the west three gates.

Greek (Byzantine Majority Text)

Ἀπὸ ἀνατολῶν, πυλῶνες τρεῖς· καὶ ἀπὸ βορρᾶ, πυλῶνες τρεῖς· καὶ ἀπὸ νότου, πυλῶνες τρεῖς· καὶ ἀπὸ δυσμῶν, πυλῶνες τρεῖς.

Douay-Rheims

On the east, three gates: and on the north, three gates: and on the south, three gates: and on the west, three gates.

Latin (Clementine Vulgate)

ab oriente portæ tres, et ab aquilone portæ tres, et ab austro portæ tres, et ab occasu portæ tres.

Arabic (Van Dyck)

مِنَ ٱلشَّرْقِ ثَلَاثَةُ أَبْوَابٍ، وَمِنَ ٱلشِّمَالِ ثَلَاثَةُ أَبْوَابٍ، وَمِنَ ٱلْجَنُوبِ ثَلَاثَةُ أَبْوَابٍ، وَمِنَ ٱلْغَرْبِ ثَلَاثَةُ أَبْوَابٍ.

↩

Revelation 21:14.

And the wall of the city had twelve foundations, and in them the names of the twelve apostles of the Lamb.

Greek (Byzantine Majority Text)

Καὶ τὸ τεῖχος τῆς πόλεως ἔχον θεμελίους δώδεκα, καὶ ἐπ’ αὐτῶν δώδεκα ὀνόματα τῶν δώδεκα ἀποστόλων τοῦ ἀρνίου.

Douay-Rheims

And the wall of the city had twelve foundations, and in them, the twelve names of the twelve apostles of the Lamb.

Latin (Clementine Vulgate)

Et murus civitatis habens fundamenta duodecim, et in ipsis duodecim nomina duodecim apostolorum Agni.

Arabic (Van Dyck)

وَسُورُ ٱلْمَدِينَةِ كَانَ لَهُ ٱثْنَا عَشَرَ أَسَاسًا، وَعَلَيْهَا أَسْمَاءُ رُسُلِ ٱلْخَرُوفِ ٱلِٱثْنَيْ عَشَرَ.

↩

Revelation 21:15.

And he that talked with me had a golden reed to measure the city, and the gates thereof, and the wall thereof.

Greek (Byzantine Majority Text)

Καὶ ὁ λαλῶν μετ’ ἐμοῦ εἶχεν μέτρον κάλαμον χρυσοῦν, ἵνα μετρήσῃ τὴν πόλιν, καὶ τοὺς πυλῶνας αὐτῆς, καὶ τὸ τεῖχος αὐτῆς.

Douay-Rheims

And he that spoke with me, had a measure of a reed of gold, to measure the city and the gates thereof, and the wall.

Latin (Clementine Vulgate)

Et qui loquebatur mecum, habebat mensuram arundineam auream, ut metiretur civitatem, et portas ejus, et murum.

Arabic (Van Dyck)

وَٱلَّذِي كَانَ يَتَكَلَّمُ مَعِي كَانَ مَعَهُ قَصَبَةٌ مِنْ ذَهَبٍ لِكَيْ يَقِيسَ ٱلْمَدِينَةَ وَأَبْوَابَهَا وَسُورَهَا.

↩

Revelation 21:16.

And the city lieth foursquare, and the length is as large as the breadth: and he measured the city with the reed, twelve thousand furlongs. The length and the breadth and the height of it are equal.

Greek (Byzantine Majority Text)

Καὶ ἡ πόλις τετράγωνος κεῖται, καὶ τὸ μῆκος αὐτῆς ὅσον τὸ πλάτος. Καὶ ἐμέτρησεν τὴν πόλιν τῷ καλάμῳ ἐπὶ σταδίους δώδεκα χιλιάδων· δώδεκα τὸ μῆκος καὶ τὸ πλάτος καὶ τὸ ὕψος αὐτῆς ἴσα ἐστίν.

Douay-Rheims

And the city lieth in a foursquare, and the length thereof is as great as the breadth: and he measured the city with the golden reed for twelve thousand furlongs, and the length and the height and the breadth thereof are equal.

Latin (Clementine Vulgate)

Et civitas in quadro posita est, et longitudo ejus tanta est quanta et latitudo: et mensus est civitatem de arundine aurea per stadia duodecim millia: et longitudo, et altitudo, et latitudo ejus æqualia sunt.

Arabic (Van Dyck)

وَٱلْمَدِينَةُ كَانَتْ مَوْضُوعَةً مُرَبَّعَةً، طُولُهَا بِقَدْرِ ٱلْعَرْضِ. فَقَاسَ ٱلْمَدِينَةَ بِٱلْقَصَبَةِ مَسَافَةَ ٱثْنَيْ عَشَرَ أَلْفَ غَلْوَةٍ. ٱلطُّولُ وَٱلْعَرْضُ وَٱلِٱرْتِفَاعُ مُتَسَاوِيَةٌ.

↩

Revelation 21:17.

And he measured the wall thereof, an hundred [and] forty [and] four cubits, [according to] the measure of a man, that is, of the angel.

Greek (Byzantine Majority Text)

Καὶ ἐμέτρησεν τὸ τεῖχος αὐτῆς ἑκατὸν τεσσαράκοντα τεσσάρων πηχῶν, μέτρον ἀνθρώπου, ὅ ἐστιν ἀγγέλου.

Douay-Rheims

And he measured the wall thereof an hundred and forty-four cubits, the measure of a man, which is of an angel.

Latin (Clementine Vulgate)

Et mensus est murum ejus centum quadraginta quatuor cubitorum, mensura hominis, quæ est angeli.

Arabic (Van Dyck)

وَقَاسَ سُورَهَا: مِئَةً وَأَرْبَعًا وَأَرْبَعِينَ ذِرَاعًا، ذِرَاعَ إِنْسَانٍ أَيِ ٱلْمَلَاكُ.

↩

Revelation 21:18.

And the building of the wall of it was [of] jasper: and the city [was] pure gold, like unto clear glass.

Greek (Byzantine Majority Text)

Καὶ ἦν ἡ ἐνδόμησις τοῦ τείχους αὐτῆς ἴασπις· καὶ ἡ πόλις χρυσίον καθαρόν, ὅμοιον ὑέλῳ καθαρῷ.

Douay-Rheims

And the building of the wall thereof was of jasper stone: but the city itself pure gold, like to clear glass.

Latin (Clementine Vulgate)

Et erat structura muri ejus ex lapide jaspide: ipsa vero civitas aurum mundum simile vitro mundo.

Arabic (Van Dyck)

وَكَانَ بِنَاءُ سُورِهَا مِنْ يَشْبٍ، وَٱلْمَدِينَةُ ذَهَبٌ نَقِيٌّ شِبْهُ زُجَاجٍ نَقِيٍّ.

↩

Revelation 21:19.

And the foundations of the wall of the city [were] garnished with all manner of precious stones. The first foundation [was] jasper; the second, sapphire; the third, a chalcedony; the fourth, an emerald;

Greek (Byzantine Majority Text)

Οἱ θεμέλιοι τοῦ τείχους τῆς πόλεως παντὶ λίθῳ τιμίῳ κεκοσμημένοι. Ὁ θεμέλιος ὁ πρῶτος, ἴασπις· ὁ δεύτερος, σάπφειρος· ὁ τρίτος, χαλκηδών· ὁ τέταρτος, σμάραγδος·

Douay-Rheims

And the foundations of the wall of the city were adorned with all manner of precious stones. The first foundation was jasper: the second, sapphire: the third, a chalcedony: the fourth, an emerald:

Latin (Clementine Vulgate)

Et fundamenta muri civitatis omni lapide pretioso ornata. Fundamentum primum, jaspis: secundum, sapphirus: tertium, calcedonius: quartum, smaragdus:

Arabic (Van Dyck)

وَأَسَاسَاتُ سُورِ ٱلْمَدِينَةِ مُزَيَّنَةٌ بِكُلِّ حَجَرٍ كَرِيمٍ. ٱلْأَسَاسُ ٱلْأَوَّلُ يَشْبٌ. ٱلثَّانِي يَاقُوتٌ أَزْرَقُ. ٱلثَّالِثُ عَقِيقٌ أَبْيَضُ. ٱلرَّابِعُ زُمُرُّدٌ ذُبَابِيٌّ

↩

Revelation 21:20.

The fifth, sardonyx; the sixth, sardius; the seventh, chrysolite; the eighth, beryl; the ninth, a topaz; the tenth, a chrysoprasus; the eleventh, a jacinth; the twelfth, an amethyst.

Greek (Byzantine Majority Text)

ὁ πέμπτος, σαρδόνυξ· ὁ ἕκτος, σάρδιον· ὁ ἕβδομος, χρυσόλιθος· ὁ ὄγδοος, βήρυλλος· ὁ ἔνατος, τοπάζιον· ὁ δέκατος, χρυσόπρασος· ὁ ἑνδέκατος, ὑάκινθος· ὁ δωδέκατος, ἀμέθυσος.

Douay-Rheims

The fifth, sardonyx: the sixth, sardius: the seventh, chrysolite: the eighth, beryl: the ninth, a topaz: the tenth, a chrysoprasus: the eleventh, a jacinth: the twelfth, an amethyst.

Latin (Clementine Vulgate)

quintum, sardonyx: sextum, sardius: septimum, chrysolithus: octavum, beryllus: nonum, topazius: decimum, chrysoprasus: undecimum, hyacinthus: duodecimum, amethystus.

Arabic (Van Dyck)

ٱلْخَامِسُ جَزَعٌ عَقِيقِيٌّ. ٱلسَّادِسُ عَقِيقٌ أَحْمَرُ. ٱلسَّابِعُ زَبَرْجَدٌ. ٱلثَّامِنُ زُمُرُّدٌ سِلْقِيٌّ. ٱلتَّاسِعُ يَاقُوتٌ أَصْفَرُ. ٱلْعَاشِرُ عَقِيقٌ أَخْضَرُ. ٱلْحَادِي عَشَرَ أَسْمَانْجُونِيٌّ. ٱلثَّانِي عَشَرَ جَمَشْتٌ.

↩

Revelation 21:21.

And the twelve gates [were] twelve pearls; every several gate was of one pearl: and the street of the city [was] pure gold, as it were transparent glass.

Greek (Byzantine Majority Text)

Καὶ οἱ δώδεκα πυλῶνες, δώδεκα μαργαρῖται· ἀνὰ εἷς ἕκαστος τῶν πυλώνων ἦν ἐξ ἑνὸς μαργαρίτου· καὶ ἡ πλατεῖα τῆς πόλεως χρυσίον καθαρόν, ὡς ὕελος διαυγής.

Douay-Rheims

And the twelve gates are twelve pearls, one to each: and every several gate was of one several pearl. And the street of the city was pure gold, as it were transparent glass.

Latin (Clementine Vulgate)

Et duodecim portæ, duodecim margaritæ sunt, per singulas: et singulæ portæ erant ex singulis margaritis: et platea civitatis aurum mundum, tamquam vitrum perlucidum.

Arabic (Van Dyck)

وَٱلِٱثْنَا عَشَرَ بَابًا ٱثْنَتَا عَشَرَةَ لُؤْلُؤَةً، كُلُّ وَاحِدٍ مِنَ ٱلْأَبْوَابِ كَانَ مِنْ لُؤْلُؤَةٍ وَاحِدَةٍ. وَسُوقُ ٱلْمَدِينَةِ ذَهَبٌ نَقِيٌّ كَزُجَاجٍ شَفَّافٍ.

↩

Revelation 21:22.

And I saw no temple therein: for the Lord God Almighty and the Lamb are the temple of it.

Greek (Byzantine Majority Text)

Καὶ ναὸν οὐκ εἶδον ἐν αὐτῇ· ὁ γὰρ κύριος ὁ θεὸς ὁ παντοκράτωρ ναὸς αὐτῆς ἐστίν, καὶ τὸ ἀρνίον.

Douay-Rheims

And I saw no temple therein. For the Lord God Almighty is the temple thereof, and the Lamb.

Latin (Clementine Vulgate)

Et templum non vidi in ea: Dominus enim Deus omnipotens templum illius est, et Agnus.

Arabic (Van Dyck)

وَلَمْ أَرَ فِيهَا هَيْكَلًا، لِأَنَّ ٱلرَّبَّ ٱللهَ ٱلْقَادِرَ عَلَى كُلِّ شَيْءٍ، هُوَ وَٱلْخَرُوفُ هَيْكَلُهَا.

↩

Revelation 21:23.

And the city had no need of the sun, neither of the moon, to shine in it: for the glory of God did lighten it, and the Lamb [is] the light thereof.

Greek (Byzantine Majority Text)

Καὶ ἡ πόλις οὐ χρείαν ἔχει τοῦ ἡλίου, οὐδὲ τῆς σελήνης, ἵνα φαίνωσιν αὐτῇ· ἡ γὰρ δόξα τοῦ θεοῦ ἐφώτισεν αὐτήν, καὶ ὁ λύχνος αὐτῆς τὸ ἀρνίον.

Douay-Rheims

And the city hath no need of the sun, nor of the moon, to shine in it. For the glory of God hath enlightened it, and the Lamb is the lamp thereof.

Latin (Clementine Vulgate)

Et civitas non eget sole neque luna ut luceant in ea, nam claritas Dei illuminavit eam, et lucerna ejus est Agnus.

Arabic (Van Dyck)

وَٱلْمَدِينَةُ لَا تَحْتَاجُ إِلَى ٱلشَّمْسِ وَلَا إِلَى ٱلْقَمَرِ لِيُضِيئَا فِيهَا، لِأَنَّ مَجْدَ ٱللهِ قَدْ أَنَارَهَا، وَٱلْخَرُوفُ سِرَاجُهَا.

↩

Revelation 21:24.

And the nations of them which are saved shall walk in the light of it: and the kings of the earth do bring their glory and honour into it.

Greek (Byzantine Majority Text)

Καὶ περιπατήσουσιν τὰ ἔθνη διὰ τοῦ φωτὸς αὐτῆς· καὶ οἱ βασιλεῖς τῆς γῆς φέρουσιν αὐτῷ δόξαν καὶ τιμὴν τῶν ἐθνῶν εἰς αὐτήν.

Douay-Rheims

And the nations shall walk in the light of it: and the kings of the earth shall bring their glory and honour into it.

Latin (Clementine Vulgate)

Et ambulabunt gentes in lumine ejus: et reges terræ afferent gloriam suam et honorem in illam.

Arabic (Van Dyck)

وَتَمْشِي شُعُوبُ ٱلْمُخَلَّصِينَ بِنُورِهَا، وَمُلُوكُ ٱلْأَرْضِ يَجِيئُونَ بِمَجْدِهِمْ وَكَرَامَتِهِمْ إِلَيْهَا.

↩

Revelation 21:25.

And the gates of it shall not be shut at all by day: for there shall be no night there.

Greek (Byzantine Majority Text)

Καὶ οἱ πυλῶνες αὐτῆς οὐ μὴ κλεισθῶσιν ἡμέρας- νὺξ γὰρ οὐκ ἔσται ἐκεῖ-

Douay-Rheims

And the gates thereof shall not be shut by day: for there shall be no night there.

Latin (Clementine Vulgate)

Et portæ ejus non claudentur per diem: nox enim non erit illic.

Arabic (Van Dyck)

وَأَبْوَابُهَا لَنْ تُغْلَقَ نَهَارًا، لِأَنَّ لَيْلًا لَا يَكُونُ هُنَاكَ.

↩

Revelation 21:26.

And they shall bring the glory and honour of the nations into it.

Greek (Byzantine Majority Text)

καὶ οἴσουσιν τὴν δόξαν καὶ τὴν τιμὴν τῶν ἐθνῶν εἰς αὐτήν·

Douay-Rheims

And they shall bring the glory and honour of the nations into it.

Latin (Clementine Vulgate)

Et afferent gloriam et honorem gentium in illam.

Arabic (Van Dyck)

وَيَجِيئُونَ بِمَجْدِ ٱلْأُمَمِ وَكَرَامَتِهِمْ إِلَيْهَا.

↩

Revelation 21:27.

And there shall in no wise enter into it any thing that defileth, neither [whatsoever] worketh abomination, or [maketh] a lie: but they which are written in the Lamb’s book of life.

Greek (Byzantine Majority Text)

καὶ οὐ μὴ εἰσέλθῃ εἰς αὐτὴν πᾶν κοινόν, καὶ ποιοῦν βδέλυγμα καὶ ψεῦδος· εἰ μὴ οἱ γεγραμμένοι ἐν τῷ βιβλίῳ τῆς ζωῆς τοῦ ἀρνίου.

Douay-Rheims

There shall not enter into it any thing defiled, or that worketh abomination or maketh a lie, but they that are written in the book of life of the Lamb.

Latin (Clementine Vulgate)

Non intrabit in eam aliquod coinquinatum, aut abominationem faciens et mendacium, nisi qui scripti sunt in libro vitæ Agni.

Arabic (Van Dyck)

وَلَنْ يَدْخُلَهَا شَيْءٌ دَنِسٌ وَلَا مَا يَصْنَعُ رَجِسًا وَكَذِبًا، إِلَّا ٱلْمَكْتُوبِينَ فِي سِفْرِ حَيَاةِ ٱلْخَرُوفِ.

↩

Revelation 22:1.

And he shewed me a pure river of water of life, clear as crystal, proceeding out of the throne of God and of the Lamb.

Greek (Byzantine Majority Text)

Καὶ ἔδειξέν μοι ποταμὸν καθαρὸν ὕδατος ζωῆς, λαμπρὸν ὡς κρύσταλλον, ἐκπορευόμενον ἐκ τοῦ θρόνου τοῦ θεοῦ καὶ τοῦ ἀρνίου.

Douay-Rheims

And he showed me a river of water of life, clear as crystal, proceeding from the throne of God and of the Lamb.

Latin (Clementine Vulgate)

Et ostendit mihi fluvium aquæ vitæ, splendidum tamquam crystallum, procedentem de sede Dei et Agni.

Arabic (Van Dyck)

وَأَرَانِي نَهْرًا صَافِيًا مِنْ مَاءِ حَيَاةٍ لَامِعًا كَبَلُّورٍ، خَارِجًا مِنْ عَرْشِ ٱللهِ وَٱلْخَرُوفِ.

↩

Revelation 22:2.

In the midst of the street of it, and on either side of the river, [was there] the tree of life, which bare twelve [manner of] fruits, [and] yielded her fruit every month: and the leaves of the tree [were] for the healing of the nations.

Greek (Byzantine Majority Text)

Ἐν μέσῳ τῆς πλατείας αὐτῆς, καὶ τοῦ ποταμοῦ ἐντεῦθεν καὶ ἐκεῖθεν, ξύλον ζωῆς, ποιοῦν καρποὺς δώδεκα, κατὰ μῆνα ἕκαστον ἀποδιδοὺς τὸν καρπὸν αὐτοῦ· καὶ τὰ φύλλα τοῦ ξύλου εἰς θεραπείαν τῶν ἐθνῶν.

Douay-Rheims

In the midst of the street thereof, and on both sides of the river, was the tree of life, bearing twelve fruits, yielding its fruits every month, and the leaves of the tree were for the healing of the nations.

Latin (Clementine Vulgate)

In medio plateæ ejus, et ex utraque parte fluminis, lignum vitæ, afferens fructus duodecim per menses singulos, reddens fructum suum et folia ligni ad sanitatem gentium.

Arabic (Van Dyck)

فِي وَسَطِ سُوقِهَا وَعَلَى ٱلنَّهْرِ مِنْ هُنَا وَمِنْ هُنَاكَ، شَجَرَةُ حَيَاةٍ تَصْنَعُ ٱثْنَتَيْ عَشْرَةَ ثَمَرَةً، وَتُعْطِي كُلَّ شَهْرٍ ثَمَرَهَا، وَوَرَقُ ٱلشَّجَرَةِ لِشِفَاءِ ٱلْأُمَمِ.

↩

Revelation 22:3.

And there shall be no more curse: but the throne of God and of the Lamb shall be in it; and his servants shall serve him:

Greek (Byzantine Majority Text)

Καὶ πᾶν κατάθεμα οὐκ ἔσται ἔτι· καὶ ὁ θρόνος τοῦ θεοῦ καὶ τοῦ ἀρνίου ἐν αὐτῇ ἔσται· καὶ οἱ δοῦλοι αὐτοῦ λατρεύσουσιν αὐτῷ,

Douay-Rheims

And there shall be no curse any more; but the throne of God and of the Lamb shall be in it, and his servants shall serve him.

Latin (Clementine Vulgate)

Et omne maledictum non erit amplius: sed sedes Dei et Agni in illa erunt, et servi ejus servient illi.

Arabic (Van Dyck)

وَلَا تَكُونُ لَعْنَةٌ مَا فِي مَا بَعْدُ. وَعَرْشُ ٱللهِ وَٱلْخَرُوفِ يَكُونُ فِيهَا، وَعَبِيدُهُ يَخْدِمُونَهُ.

↩

Revelation 22:4.

And they shall see his face; and his name [shall be] in their foreheads.

Greek (Byzantine Majority Text)

καὶ ὄψονται τὸ πρόσωπον αὐτοῦ· καὶ τὸ ὄνομα αὐτοῦ ἐπὶ τῶν μετώπων αὐτῶν.

Douay-Rheims

And they shall see his face: and his name shall be on their foreheads.

Latin (Clementine Vulgate)

Et videbunt faciem ejus: et nomen ejus in frontibus eorum.

Arabic (Van Dyck)

وَهُمْ سَيَنْظُرُونَ وَجْهَهُ، وَٱسْمُهُ عَلَى جِبَاهِهِمْ.

↩

Revelation 22:5.

And there shall be no night there; and they need no candle, neither light of the sun; for the Lord God giveth them light: and they shall reign for ever and ever.

Greek (Byzantine Majority Text)

Καὶ νὺξ οὐκ ἔσται ἐκεῖ, καὶ χρείαν οὐκ ἔχουσιν λύχνου καὶ φωτὸς ἡλίου, ὅτι κύριος ὁ θεὸς φωτιεῖ αὐτούς· καὶ βασιλεύσουσιν εἰς τοὺς αἰῶνας τῶν αἰώνων.

Douay-Rheims

And night shall be no more: and they shall not need the light of the lamp, nor the light of the sun, because the Lord God shall enlighten them, and they shall reign for ever and ever.

Latin (Clementine Vulgate)

Et nox ultra non erit: et non egebunt lumine lucernæ, neque lumine solis, quoniam Dominus Deus illuminabit illos, et regnabunt in sæcula sæculorum.

Arabic (Van Dyck)

وَلَا يَكُونُ لَيْلٌ هُنَاكَ، وَلَا يَحْتَاجُونَ إِلَى سِرَاجٍ أَوْ نُورِ شَمْسٍ، لِأَنَّ ٱلرَّبَّ ٱلْإِلَهَ يُنِيرُ عَلَيْهِمْ، وَهُمْ سَيَمْلِكُونَ إِلَى أَبَدِ ٱلْآبِدِينَ.

↩

Revelation 22:6.

And he said unto me, These sayings [are] faithful and true: and the Lord God of the holy prophets sent his angel to shew unto his servants the things which must shortly be done.

Greek (Byzantine Majority Text)

Καὶ λέγει μοι, Οὗτοι οἱ λόγοι πιστοὶ καὶ ἀληθινοί· καὶ κύριος ὁ θεὸς τῶν πνευμάτων τῶν προφητῶν ἀπέστειλεν τὸν ἄγγελον αὐτοῦ δεῖξαι τοῖς δούλοις αὐτοῦ ἃ δεῖ γενέσθαι ἐν τάχει.

Douay-Rheims

And he said to me: These words are most faithful and true. And the Lord God of the spirits of the prophets sent his angel to shew his servants the things which must be done shortly.

Latin (Clementine Vulgate)

Et dixit mihi: Hæc verba fidelissima sunt, et vera. Et Dominus Deus spirituum prophetarum misit angelum suum ostendere servis suis quæ oportet fieri cito.

Arabic (Van Dyck)

ثُمَّ قَالَ لِي: «هَذِهِ ٱلْأَقْوَالُ أَمِينَةٌ وَصَادِقَةٌ. وَٱلرَّبُّ إِلَهُ ٱلْأَنْبِيَاءِ ٱلْقِدِّيسِينَ أَرْسَلَ مَلَاكَهُ لِيُرِيَ عَبِيدَهُ مَا يَنْبَغِي أَنْ يَكُونَ سَرِيعًا».

↩

Revelation 22:7.

Behold, I come quickly: blessed [is] he that keepeth the sayings of the prophecy of this book.

Greek (Byzantine Majority Text)

Καὶ ἰδού, ἔρχομαι ταχύ. Μακάριος ὁ τηρῶν τοὺς λόγους τῆς προφητείας τοῦ βιβλίου τούτου.

Douay-Rheims

And, Behold I come quickly. Blessed is he that keepeth the words of the prophecy of this book.

Latin (Clementine Vulgate)

Et ecce venio velociter. Beatus, qui custodit verba prophetiæ libri hujus.

Arabic (Van Dyck)

«هَا أَنَا آتِي سَرِيعًا. طُوبَى لِمَنْ يَحْفَظُ أَقْوَالَ نُبُوَّةِ هَذَا ٱلْكِتَابِ».

↩

Revelation 22:8.

And I John saw these things, and heard [them]. And when I had heard and seen, I fell down to worship before the feet of the angel which shewed me these things.

Greek (Byzantine Majority Text)

Κἀγὼ Ἰωάννης ὁ ἀκούων καὶ βλέπων ταῦτα. Καὶ ὅτε ἤκουσα καὶ ἔβλεψα, ἔπεσον προσκυνῆσαι ἔμπροσθεν τῶν ποδῶν τοῦ ἀγγέλου τοῦ δεικνύοντός μοι ταῦτα.

Douay-Rheims

And I, John, who have heard and seen these things. And after I had heard and seen, I fell down to adore before the feet of the angel, who shewed me these things.

Latin (Clementine Vulgate)

Et ego Joannes, qui audivi, et vidi hæc. Et postquam audissem, et vidissem, cecidi ut adorarem ante pedes angeli, qui mihi hæc ostendebat:

Arabic (Van Dyck)

وَأَنَا يُوحَنَّا ٱلَّذِي كَانَ يَنْظُرُ وَيَسْمَعُ هَذَا. وَحِينَ سَمِعْتُ وَنَظَرْتُ، خَرَرْتُ لِأَسْجُدَ أَمَامَ رِجْلَيِ ٱلْمَلَاكِ ٱلَّذِي كَانَ يُرِينِي هَذَا.

↩

Revelation 22:9.

Then saith he unto me, See [thou do it] not: for I am thy fellowservant, and of thy brethren the prophets, and of them which keep the sayings of this book: worship God.

Greek (Byzantine Majority Text)

Καὶ λέγει μοι, Ὅρα μή· σύνδουλός σού εἰμι, καὶ τῶν ἀδελφῶν σου τῶν προφητῶν, καὶ τῶν τηρούντων τοὺς λόγους τοῦ βιβλίου τούτου· τῷ θεῷ προσκύνησον.

Douay-Rheims

And he said to me: See thou do it not: for I am thy fellow servant, and of thy brethren the prophets, and of them that keep the words of the prophecy of this book. Adore God.

Latin (Clementine Vulgate)

et dixit mihi: Vide ne feceris: conservus enim tuus sum, et fratrum tuorum prophetarum, et eorum qui servant verba prophetiæ libri hujus: Deum adora.

Arabic (Van Dyck)

فَقَالَ لِيَ: «ٱنْظُرْ لَا تَفْعَلْ! لِأَنِّي عَبْدٌ مَعَكَ وَمَعَ إِخْوَتِكَ ٱلْأَنْبِيَاءِ، وَٱلَّذِينَ يَحْفَظُونَ أَقْوَالَ هَذَا ٱلْكِتَابِ. ٱسْجُدْ لِلهِ!».

↩

Revelation 22:10.

And he saith unto me, Seal not the sayings of the prophecy of this book: for the time is at hand.

Greek (Byzantine Majority Text)

Καὶ λέγει μοι, Μὴ σφραγίσῃς τοὺς λόγους τῆς προφητείας τοῦ βιβλίου τούτου· ὁ καιρὸς γὰρ ἐγγύς ἐστιν.

Douay-Rheims

And he saith to me: Seal not the words of the prophecy of this book: for the time is at hand.

Latin (Clementine Vulgate)

Et dicit mihi: Ne signaveris verba prophetiæ libri hujus: tempus enim prope est.

Arabic (Van Dyck)

وَقَالَ لِي: «لَا تَخْتِمْ عَلَى أَقْوَالِ نُبُوَّةِ هَذَا ٱلْكِتَابِ، لِأَنَّ ٱلْوَقْتَ قَرِيبٌ.

↩

Revelation 22:11.

He that is unjust, let him be unjust still: and he which is filthy, let him be filthy still: and he that is righteous, let him be righteous still: and he that is holy, let him be holy still.

Greek (Byzantine Majority Text)

Ὁ ἀδικῶν ἀδικησάτω ἔτι· καὶ ὁ ῥυπαρὸς ῥυπαρευθήτω ἔτι· καὶ ὁ δίκαιος δικαιοσύνην ποιησάτω ἔτι· καὶ ὁ ἅγιος ἁγιασθήτω ἔτι.

Douay-Rheims

He that hurteth, let him hurt still: and he that is filthy, let him be filthy still: and he that is just, let him be justified still: and he that is holy, let him be sanctified still.

Latin (Clementine Vulgate)

Qui nocet, noceat adhuc: et qui in sordibus est, sordescat adhuc: et qui justus est, justificetur adhuc: et sanctus, sanctificetur adhuc.

Arabic (Van Dyck)

مَنْ يَظْلِمْ فَلْيَظْلِمْ بَعْدُ. وَمَنْ هُوَ نَجِسٌ فَلْيَتَنَجَّسْ بَعْدُ. وَمَنْ هُوَ بَارٌّ فَلْيَتَبَرَّرْ بَعْدُ. وَمَنْ هُوَ مُقَدَّسٌ فَلْيَتَقَدَّسْ بَعْدُ».

↩

Revelation 22:12.

And, behold, I come quickly; and my reward [is] with me, to give every man according as his work shall be.

Greek (Byzantine Majority Text)

Ἰδού, ἔρχομαι ταχύ, καὶ ὁ μισθός μου μετ’ ἐμοῦ, ἀποδοῦναι ἑκάστῳ ὡς τὸ ἔργον ἔσται αὐτοῦ.

Douay-Rheims

Behold, I come quickly; and my reward is with me, to render to every man according to his works.

Latin (Clementine Vulgate)

Ecce venio cito, et merces mea mecum est, reddere unicuique secundum opera sua.

Arabic (Van Dyck)

«وَهَا أَنَا آتِي سَرِيعًا وَأُجْرَتِي مَعِي لِأُجَازِيَ كُلَّ وَاحِدٍ كَمَا يَكُونُ عَمَلُهُ.

↩

Revelation 22:13.

I am Alpha and Omega, the beginning and the end, the first and the last.

Greek (Byzantine Majority Text)

Ἐγὼ τὸ Ἄλφα καὶ τὸ Ὦ, ὁ πρῶτος καὶ ὁ ἔσχατος, ἡ ἀρχὴ καὶ τὸ τέλος.

Douay-Rheims

I am Alpha and Omega, the first and the last, the beginning and the end.

Latin (Clementine Vulgate)

Ego sum alpha et omega, primus et novissimus, principium et finis.

Arabic (Van Dyck)

أَنَا ٱلْأَلِفُ وَٱلْيَاءُ، ٱلْبِدَايَةُ وَٱلنِّهَايَةُ، ٱلْأَوَّلُ وَٱلْآخِرُ».

↩

Revelation 22:14.

Blessed [are] they that do his commandments, that they may have right to the tree of life, and may enter in through the gates into the city.

Greek (Byzantine Majority Text)

Μακάριοι οἱ ποιοῦντες τὰς ἐντολὰς αὐτοῦ, ἵνα ἔσται ἡ ἐξουσία αὐτῶν ἐπὶ τὸ ξύλον τῆς ζωῆς, καὶ τοῖς πυλῶσιν εἰσέλθωσιν εἰς τὴν πόλιν.

Douay-Rheims

Blessed are they that wash their robes in the blood of the Lamb: that they may have a right to the tree of life, and may enter in by the gates into the city.

Latin (Clementine Vulgate)

Beati, qui lavant stolas suas in sanguine Agni: ut sit potestas eorum in ligno vitæ, et per portas intrent in civitatem.

Arabic (Van Dyck)

طُوبَى لِلَّذِينَ يَصْنَعُونَ وَصَايَاهُ لِكَيْ يَكُونَ سُلْطَانُهُمْ عَلَى شَجَرَةِ ٱلْحَيَاةِ، وَيَدْخُلُوا مِنَ ٱلْأَبْوَابِ إِلَى ٱلْمَدِينَةِ،

↩

Revelation 22:15.

For without [are] dogs, and sorcerers, and whoremongers, and murderers, and idolaters, and whosoever loveth and maketh a lie.

Greek (Byzantine Majority Text)

Ἔξω οἱ κύνες καὶ οἱ φαρμακοὶ καὶ οἱ πόρνοι καὶ οἱ φονεῖς καὶ οἱ εἰδωλολάτραι, καὶ πᾶς φιλῶν καὶ ποιῶν ψεῦδος.

Douay-Rheims

Without are dogs, and sorcerers, and unchaste, and murderers, and servers of idols, and every one that loveth and maketh a lie.

Latin (Clementine Vulgate)

Foris canes, et venefici, et impudici, et homicidæ, et idolis servientes, et omnis qui amat et facit mendacium.

Arabic (Van Dyck)

لِأَنَّ خَارِجًا ٱلْكِلَابَ وَٱلسَّحَرَةَ وَٱلزُّنَاةَ وَٱلْقَتَلَةَ وَعَبَدَةَ ٱلْأَوْثَانِ، وَكُلَّ مَنْ يُحِبُّ وَيَصْنَعُ كَذِبًا.

↩

Revelation 22:16.

I Jesus have sent mine angel to testify unto you these things in the churches. I am the root and the offspring of David, [and] the bright and morning star.

Greek (Byzantine Majority Text)

Ἐγὼ Ἰησοῦς ἔπεμψα τὸν ἄγγελόν μου μαρτυρῆσαι ὑμῖν ταῦτα ἐπὶ ταῖς ἐκκλησίαις. Ἐγώ εἰμι ἡ ῥίζα καὶ τὸ γένος Δαυίδ, ὁ ἀστὴρ ὁ λαμπρὸς ὁ πρωϊνός.

Douay-Rheims

I Jesus have sent my angel, to testify to you these things in the churches. I am the root and stock of David, the bright and morning star.

Latin (Clementine Vulgate)

Ego Jesus misi angelum meum testificari vobis hæc in ecclesiis. Ego sum radix, et genus David, stella splendida et matutina.

Arabic (Van Dyck)

«أَنَا يَسُوعُ، أَرْسَلْتُ مَلَاكِي لِأَشْهَدَ لَكُمْ بِهَذِهِ ٱلْأُمُورِ عَنِ ٱلْكَنَائِسِ. أَنَا أَصْلُ وَذُرِّيَّةُ دَاوُدَ. كَوْكَبُ ٱلصُّبْحِ ٱلْمُنِيرُ».

↩

Revelation 22:17.

And the Spirit and the bride say, Come. And let him that heareth say, Come. And let him that is athirst come. And whosoever will, let him take the water of life freely.

Greek (Byzantine Majority Text)

Καὶ τὸ πνεῦμα καὶ ἡ νύμφη λέγουσιν, Ἔρχου. Καὶ ὁ ἀκούων εἰπάτω, Ἔρχου. Καὶ ὁ διψῶν ἐρχέσθω· ὁ θέλων λαβέτω ὕδωρ ζωῆς δωρεάν.

Douay-Rheims

And the spirit and the bride say: Come. And he that heareth, let him say: Come. And he that thirsteth, let him come: and he that will, let him take the water of life, freely.

Latin (Clementine Vulgate)

Et spiritus, et sponsa dicunt: Veni. Et qui audit, dicat: Veni. Et qui sitit, veniat: et qui vult, accipiat aquam vitæ, gratis.

Arabic (Van Dyck)

وَٱلرُّوحُ وَٱلْعَرُوسُ يَقُولَانِ: «تَعَالَ!». وَمَنْ يَسْمَعْ فَلْيَقُلْ: «تَعَالَ!». وَمَنْ يَعْطَشْ فَلْيَأْتِ. وَمَنْ يُرِدْ فَلْيَأْخُذْ مَاءَ حَيَاةٍ مَجَّانًا.

↩

Revelation 22:18.

For I testify unto every man that heareth the words of the prophecy of this book, If any man shall add unto these things, God shall add unto him the plagues that are written in this book:

Greek (Byzantine Majority Text)

Μαρτυρῶ ἐγὼ παντὶ τῷ ἀκούοντι τοὺς λόγους τῆς προφητείας τοῦ βιβλίου τούτου, ἐάν τις ἐπιθῇ ἐπ’ αὐτά, ἐπιθήσαι ὁ θεὸς ἐπ’ αὐτὸν τὰς πληγὰς τὰς γεγραμμένας ἐν τῷ βιβλίῳ τούτῳ·

Douay-Rheims

For I testify to every one that heareth the words of the prophecy of this book: If any man shall add to these things, God shall add unto him the plagues written in this book.

Latin (Clementine Vulgate)

Contestor enim omni audienti verba prophetiæ libri hujus: si quis apposuerit ad hæc, apponet Deus super illum plagas scriptas in libro isto.

Arabic (Van Dyck)

لِأَنِّي أَشْهَدُ لِكُلِّ مَنْ يَسْمَعُ أَقْوَالَ نُبُوَّةِ هَذَا ٱلْكِتَابِ: إِنْ كَانَ أَحَدٌ يَزِيدُ عَلَى هَذَا، يَزِيدُ ٱللهُ عَلَيْهِ ٱلضَّرَبَاتِ ٱلْمَكْتُوبَةَ فِي هَذَا ٱلْكِتَابِ.

↩

Revelation 22:19.

And if any man shall take away from the words of the book of this prophecy, God shall take away his part out of the book of life, and out of the holy city, and [from] the things which are written in this book.

Greek (Byzantine Majority Text)

καὶ ἐάν τις ἀφέλῃ ἀπὸ τῶν λόγων τοῦ βιβλίου τῆς προφητείας ταύτης, ἀφέλοι ὁ θεὸς τὸ μέρος αὐτοῦ ἀπὸ τοῦ ξύλου τῆς ζωῆς, καὶ ἐκ τῆς πόλεως τῆς ἁγίας, τῶν γεγραμμένων ἐν τῷ βιβλίῳ τούτῳ.

Douay-Rheims

And if any man shall take away from the words of the book of this prophecy, God shall take away his part out of the book of life, and out of the holy city, and from these things that are written in this book.

Latin (Clementine Vulgate)

Et si quis diminuerit de verbis libri prophetiæ hujus, auferet Deus partem ejus de libro vitæ, et de civitate sancta, et de his quæ scripta sunt in libro isto:

Arabic (Van Dyck)

وَإِنْ كَانَ أَحَدٌ يَحْذِفُ مِنْ أَقْوَالِ كِتَابِ هَذِهِ ٱلنُّبُوَّةِ، يَحْذِفُ ٱللهُ نَصِيبَهُ مِنْ سِفْرِ ٱلْحَيَاةِ، وَمِنَ ٱلْمَدِينَةِ ٱلْمُقَدَّسَةِ، وَمِنَ ٱلْمَكْتُوبِ فِي هَذَا ٱلْكِتَابِ.

↩

Revelation 22:20.

He which testifieth these things saith, Surely I come quickly. Amen. Even so, come, Lord Jesus.

Greek (Byzantine Majority Text)

Λέγει ὁ μαρτυρῶν ταῦτα, Ναί, ἔρχομαι ταχύ. Ἀμήν. Ναί, ἔρχου, κύριε Ἰησοῦ.

Douay-Rheims

He that giveth testimony of these things, saith, Surely I come quickly: Amen. Come, Lord Jesus.

Latin (Clementine Vulgate)

dicit qui testimonium perhibet istorum. Etiam venio cito: amen. Veni, Domine Jesu.

Arabic (Van Dyck)

يَقُولُ ٱلشَّاهِدُ بِهَذَا: «نَعَمْ! أَنَا آتِي سَرِيعًا». آمِينَ. تَعَالَ أَيُّهَا ٱلرَّبُّ يَسُوعُ.

↩

Revelation 22:21.

The grace of our Lord Jesus Christ [be] with you all. Amen.

Greek (Byzantine Majority Text)

Ἡ χάρις τοῦ κυρίου Ἰησοῦ χριστοῦ μετὰ πάντων τῶν ἁγίων. Ἀμήν.

Douay-Rheims

The grace of our Lord Jesus Christ be with you all. Amen.

Latin (Clementine Vulgate)

Gratia Domini nostri Jesu Christi cum omnibus vobis. Amen.

Arabic (Van Dyck)

نِعْمَةُ رَبِّنَا يَسُوعَ ٱلْمَسِيحِ مَعَ جَمِيعِكُمْ. آمِينَ.

↩
